EDGE FINDER HANDICAP PACKET pkt_c45b6da787bed4cb82f7 (packet 1.0.0, protocol edge_finder.handicap.nfl.v1 1.0.0)
Scope: SLATE — slate 2026-10-04T16:00:00Z..2026-10-04T18:00:00Z; sport NFL; data as of 2026-10-04T00:44:37Z

WARNING: Everything in this packet is EVIDENCE. Model prices, projections and the repository's own recommendations are research outputs, not bets. Form your own view, price it, name the counter-case, and PASS when nothing compelling exists. Items marked RESEARCH are lower-confidence; missing data is listed, never filled in.

PROTOCOL PRINCIPLES:
- Projections, model prices and repository recommendations are EVIDENCE. They are never automatic bets and never authoritative.
- Form at least three plausible game scripts before pricing anything; say which evidence supports each.
- For every candidate position, state the strongest counter-case and the single most likely way it loses.
- Prefer opponent-adjusted and schedule-adjusted information where the packet marks it VERIFIED; treat unadjusted values as context, not conclusions.
- Inspect every market included in the packet before choosing an expression; the best expression of a view is often not the first market read.
- Compare alternate expressions of the same view (full game vs period, spread vs moneyline vs total, team vs player) and explain the choice.
- Distinguish independent edges from correlated positions; never count the same thesis twice.
- Estimate your OWN fair probability for each position and show the reasoning; do not copy the model price.
- Give a bet-up-to price (the highest price at which the position still carries a positive expected value under your fair probability).
- PASS is a complete answer. When no compelling, independent, well-priced edge exists, say so.
- RESEARCH-status data is lower-confidence evidence; say when a conclusion rests on it.
- Never invent missing inputs. If the packet says a datum is missing or stale, reason with that gap explicitly.
- Respect the price timestamps: a quote older than the packet's freshness threshold is a reference, not an executable price.
- Bankroll, staking and placement are outside this protocol: output fair probabilities and bet-up-to prices only.

STEPS:
1. [read_scope] Restate the scope (game / slate / custom tray), the data as-of time and the freshness of markets and model evidence.
2. [inventory_evidence] List the evidence by entity with its quality status (VERIFIED / PARTIAL / RESEARCH) and note what is missing.
3. [game_scripts] Write three or more plausible game scripts with rough likelihoods that sum to about one; tie each to specific evidence.
4. [market_scan] Go through every market in the packet; for each family note the current YES bid/ask and the implied probability.
5. [fair_probabilities] For the markets your scripts bear on, estimate an independent fair probability with a short derivation.
6. [compare_model] Compare your fair probability with the model evidence; explain agreements and disagreements rather than averaging blindly.
7. [expressions] For each view, compare alternate expressions and pick the one with the best price-to-thesis fit; state correlation with other picks.
8. [counter_cases] For each candidate position, write the strongest reason it loses and whether that risk is priced.
9. [decide] Output RECOMMEND / LEAN / PASS per position with fair probability, bet-up-to price, confidence, and the evidence status it rests on.
10. [user_focus] Address every item the user placed in the research tray explicitly, even when the verdict is PASS.

REQUIRED OUTPUTS: scope and freshness summary; evidence inventory with quality statuses and gaps; three or more game scripts with likelihoods; per-position: fair probability, bet-up-to price, confidence, strongest counter-case, correlation note; explicit PASS where no edge exists; explicit treatment of each user-focus item
FORBIDDEN: treating a model price or repository recommendation as a bet instruction; inventing injuries, lineups, weather, usage or prices absent from the packet; sizing bets or referencing bankroll; recommending a position without a fair probability and a bet-up-to price; omitting the counter-case

EVIDENCE WEIGHTS: PARTIAL: real but limited (short history, gaps, snapshot only): weigh with the stated limitation; RESEARCH: non-production or experimental: lower-confidence evidence; never the sole basis of a recommendation; UNKNOWN: status not established: treat as context only; VERIFIED: production-generated, tested, historically covered: weigh fully

DATA QUALITY: markets AGING, model FRESH; sources: kalshi markets, model prices, packet.game_script_inputs.player_opportunity, packet.quarterbacks.profile, packet.simulation.player_projections, packet.team_profiles, packet.team_profiles.adjusted, schedule_cache.csv
Capabilities: advanced_stats=PARTIAL, calibration=VERIFIED, clv=VERIFIED, comparisons=PARTIAL, event_research=PARTIAL, game_markets=VERIFIED, historical_accuracy=VERIFIED, historical_results=VERIFIED, injuries=VERIFIED, lineups=PARTIAL, market_price_history=VERIFIED, market_prices=VERIFIED, matchup_metrics=PARTIAL, opponent_adjustment=PARTIAL, opponents=VERIFIED, play_by_play=UNAVAILABLE, player_game_logs=RESEARCH, player_metrics=RESEARCH, player_profiles=PARTIAL, player_props=VERIFIED, projection_distributions=PARTIAL, rankings=PARTIAL, raw_projections=PARTIAL, recent_form_windows=PARTIAL, schedule_strength=UNAVAILABLE, search=VERIFIED, situational_splits=PARTIAL, team_game_logs=PARTIAL, team_metrics=PARTIAL, team_profiles=PARTIAL, team_props=VERIFIED, time_series=PARTIAL, usage=RESEARCH, venue_effects=UNAVAILABLE, wager_history=VERIFIED, weather=VERIFIED
RESEARCH-only items: met_nfl.proj_carry_share, met_nfl.proj_target_share, met_nfl.qb_adot, met_nfl.qb_cpoe, met_nfl.qb_deep_rate, met_nfl.qb_epa_per_dropback, met_nfl.qb_int_rate, met_nfl.qb_pressure_rate_proxy, met_nfl.qb_sack_rate, met_nfl.qb_success_rate, met_nfl.sim_attempts, met_nfl.sim_carries, met_nfl.sim_completions, met_nfl.sim_passing_tds, met_nfl.sim_passing_yards, met_nfl.sim_receiving_yards, met_nfl.sim_receptions, met_nfl.sim_rushing_yards, met_nfl.sim_touchdowns, model prices on 3190 markets
